```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es" data-theme="dark">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>FazLuiz 3D — Plataforma SaaS Universal</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/jspdf/2.5.1/jspdf.umd.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/Chart.js/4.4.0/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
<style>
/* ============================================
   FAZLUIZ 3D - UNIVERSAL STYLESHEET
   Clean, Modern, Glassmorphism Design
   ============================================ */

:root {
  /* Color System */
  --bg-primary: #0F172A;
  --bg-secondary: #1E293B;
  --bg-tertiary: #334155;
  --bg-card: rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.6);
  --bg-glass: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.7);
  
  --blue-electric: #2D7FF9;
  --blue-dark: #1E40AF;
  --cyan-holo: #22D3EE;
  --cyan-light: #67E8F9;
  --white: #F8FAFC;
  --gray-100: #F1F5F9;
  --gray-200: #E2E8F0;
  --gray-300: #CBD5E1;
  --gray-400: #94A3B8;
  --gray-500: #64748B;
  --gray-600: #475569;
  --gray-700: #334155;
  
  --success: #10B981;
  --success-light: #34D399;
  --warning: #F59E0B;
  --warning-light: #FBBF24;
  --danger: #EF4444;
  --danger-light: #F87171;
  --info: #3B82F6;
  
  /* Glass Effects */
  --glass-bg: rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.4);
  --glass-border: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.15);
  --glass-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
  --glow-blue: 0 0 20px rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.3);
  --glow-cyan: 0 0 20px rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.3);
  
  /* Typography */
  --font-title: 'Space Grotesk', -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --font-body: 'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
  
  /* Spacing */
  --space-xs: 4px;
  --space-sm: 8px;
  --space-md: 16px;
  --space-lg: 24px;
  --space-xl: 32px;
  --space-2xl: 48px;
  
  /* Border Radius */
  --radius-sm: 6px;
  --radius-md: 10px;
  --radius-lg: 16px;
  --radius-xl: 24px;
  
  /* Transitions */
  --transition-fast: 0.15s ease;
  --transition-normal: 0.3s ease;
  --transition-slow: 0.5s ease;
}

[data-theme="light"] {
  --bg-primary: #F8FAFC;
  --bg-secondary: #F1F5F9;
  --bg-tertiary: #E2E8F0;
  --bg-card: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8);
  --bg-glass: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
  --glass-bg: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
  --glass-border: rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.2);
  --glass-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  --white: #0F172A;
  --gray-400: #64748B;
  --gray-500: #475569;
}

/* ============================================
   RESET & BASE
   ============================================ */
* {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html {
  scroll-behavior: smooth;
}

body {
  font-family: var(--font-body);
  background: var(--bg-primary);
  color: var(--white);
  min-height: 100vh;
  line-height: 1.6;
  overflow-x: hidden;
}

/* ============================================
   BACKGROUND EFFECTS
   ============================================ */
.bg-grid {
  position: fixed;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  background: 
    radial-gradient(ellipse at 20% 50%, rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.08) 0%, transparent 50%),
    radial-gradient(ellipse at 80% 20%, rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.06) 0%, transparent 50%),
    radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 80%, rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.04) 0%, transparent 50%);
  z-index: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.bg-grid::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  background-image: 
    linear-gradient(rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.03) 1px, transparent 1px),
    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.03) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: 50px 50px;
}

/* ============================================
   SCROLLBAR
   ============================================ */
::-webkit-scrollbar {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
  background: transparent;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: 3px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
  background: var(--cyan-holo);
}

/* ============================================
   LOGIN SCREEN
   ============================================ */
.login-screen {
  position: fixed;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  z-index: 10000;
  background: var(--bg-primary);
}

.login-container {
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
  padding: var(--space-2xl);
  width: 420px;
  max-width: 90vw;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: var(--glow-blue);
  animation: fadeIn 0.5s ease;
}

.login-logo {
  width: 80px;
  height: 80px;
  margin: 0 auto var(--space-lg);
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  font-size: 32px;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  color: white;
  box-shadow: var(--glow-cyan);
}

.login-container h1 {
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  font-size: 28px;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-sm);
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
  background-clip: text;
}

.login-container p {
  color: var(--gray-400);
  margin-bottom: var(--space-xl);
  font-size: 14px;
}

/* ============================================
   FORM ELEMENTS
   ============================================ */
.form-input, .form-select, .form-textarea {
  width: 100%;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  color: var(--white);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-family: var(--font-body);
  transition: all var(--transition-normal);
}

.form-input:focus, .form-select:focus, .form-textarea:focus {
  outline: none;
  border-color: var(--cyan-holo);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.1);
}

.form-input::placeholder {
  color: var(--gray-500);
}

.form-label {
  display: block;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 500;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-sm);
  color: var(--gray-400);
}

.form-group {
  margin-bottom: var(--space-md);
}

.form-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr));
  gap: var(--space-md);
}

/* ============================================
   BUTTONS
   ============================================ */
.btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: var(--space-sm);
  padding: 10px 20px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 500;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all var(--transition-normal);
  border: none;
  font-family: var(--font-body);
  white-space: nowrap;
  text-decoration: none;
}

.btn:disabled {
  opacity: 0.5;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.btn-primary {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  color: white;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 12px rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.3);
}

.btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.4);
}

.btn-secondary {
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  color: var(--white);
}

.btn-secondary:hover:not(:disabled) {
  border-color: var(--cyan-holo);
  background: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.1);
}

.btn-danger {
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15);
  color: var(--danger);
  border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
}

.btn-danger:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.25);
}

.btn-success {
  background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15);
  color: var(--success);
  border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3);
}

.btn-sm {
  padding: 6px 14px;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.btn-lg {
  padding: 14px 28px;
  font-size: 16px;
}

.btn-icon {
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  padding: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   LAYOUT
   ============================================ */
.app-container {
  display: none;
  min-height: 100vh;
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
}

.app-container.active {
  display: flex;
}

/* Sidebar */
.sidebar {
  width: 280px;
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  border-right: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  padding: var(--space-lg) var(--space-md);
  position: fixed;
  left: 0;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 100;
  overflow-y: auto;
  transition: transform var(--transition-normal);
}

.sidebar-logo {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-md);
  padding: var(--space-sm) var(--space-md);
  margin-bottom: var(--space-xl);
}

.sidebar-logo-icon {
  width: 44px;
  height: 44px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 18px;
  color: white;
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.sidebar-logo-text h2 {
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  font-size: 16px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
  background-clip: text;
}

.sidebar-logo-text span {
  font-size: 11px;
  color: var(--gray-400);
}

.nav-section {
  margin-bottom: var(--space-lg);
}

.nav-section-title {
  font-size: 10px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 1.5px;
  color: var(--gray-500);
  padding: 0 var(--space-md);
  margin-bottom: var(--space-sm);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.nav-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-md);
  padding: 11px var(--space-md);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all var(--transition-fast);
  font-size: 14px;
  color: var(--gray-400);
  margin-bottom: 2px;
  position: relative;
}

.nav-item:hover {
  background: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.08);
  color: var(--white);
}

.nav-item.active {
  background: rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.15);
  color: var(--cyan-holo);
  border: 1px solid rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.2);
}

.nav-item.active::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  top: 50%;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
  width: 3px;
  height: 20px;
  background: var(--cyan-holo);
  border-radius: 0 3px 3px 0;
}

.nav-icon {
  width: 20px;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 16px;
}

.nav-badge {
  margin-left: auto;
  background: var(--blue-electric);
  color: white;
  font-size: 10px;
  padding: 2px 7px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

/* Main Content */
.main-content {
  margin-left: 280px;
  flex: 1;
  padding: var(--space-lg) var(--space-xl);
  min-height: 100vh;
}

/* Top Bar */
.top-bar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-xl);
  padding-bottom: var(--space-md);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
}

.top-bar-left h1 {
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  font-size: 24px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.top-bar-left p {
  color: var(--gray-400);
  font-size: 13px;
  margin-top: var(--space-xs);
}

.top-bar-right {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-md);
}

.top-btn {
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  cursor: pointer;
  color: var(--gray-400);
  font-size: 18px;
  transition: all var(--transition-fast);
  position: relative;
}

.top-btn:hover {
  border-color: var(--cyan-holo);
  color: var(--cyan-holo);
}

.top-btn .notif-dot {
  position: absolute;
  top: 8px;
  right: 8px;
  width: 8px;
  height: 8px;
  background: var(--danger);
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.user-avatar {
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 14px;
  color: white;
  cursor: pointer;
}

/* ============================================
   CARDS & CONTAINERS
   ============================================ */
.card {
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  padding: var(--space-lg);
  margin-bottom: var(--space-md);
  transition: all var(--transition-normal);
  position: relative;
}

.card:hover {
  border-color: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.3);
}

.card-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-lg);
}

.card-title {
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  font-size: 16px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.card-subtitle {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--gray-400);
  margin-top: var(--space-xs);
}

/* Stats Grid */
.stats-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr));
  gap: var(--space-md);
  margin-bottom: var(--space-lg);
}

.stat-card {
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  padding: var(--space-lg);
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  transition: all var(--transition-normal);
}

.stat-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: var(--glow-blue);
}

.stat-card::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  right: 0;
  width: 80px;
  height: 80px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.1) 0%, transparent 70%);
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--gray-400);
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-sm);
}

.stat-value {
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  font-size: 28px;
  font-weight: 700;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--white), var(--cyan-holo));
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
  background-clip: text;
}

.stat-change {
  font-size: 12px;
  margin-top: var(--space-sm);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-xs);
}

.stat-change.positive { color: var(--success); }
.stat-change.negative { color: var(--danger); }

/* Grid Layouts */
.grid-2 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  gap: var(--space-lg);
}

.grid-3 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: var(--space-lg);
}

.grid-4 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
  gap: var(--space-md);
}

/* ============================================
   TABLES
   ============================================ */
.table-responsive {
  overflow-x: auto;
}

.data-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
}

.data-table th {
  text-align: left;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  font-size: 11px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 1px;
  color: var(--gray-500);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  font-weight: 600;
}

.data-table td {
  padding: 14px 16px;
  font-size: 14px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.05);
}

.data-table tr:hover td {
  background: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.03);
}

/* ============================================
   BADGES
   ============================================ */
.badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 4px 12px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.badge-success { 
  background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.15); 
  color: var(--success); 
  border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3);
}

.badge-warning { 
  background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15); 
  color: var(--warning); 
  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3);
}

.badge-danger { 
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15); 
  color: var(--danger); 
  border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3);
}

.badge-info { 
  background: rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.15); 
  color: var(--info); 
  border: 1px solid rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.3);
}

.badge-cyan { 
  background: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.15); 
  color: var(--cyan-holo); 
  border: 1px solid rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.3);
}

/* ============================================
   MODALS
   ============================================ */
.modal-overlay {
  position: fixed;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
  backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
  z-index: 1000;
  display: none;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: var(--space-md);
}

.modal-overlay.active {
  display: flex;
  animation: fadeIn 0.2s ease;
}

.modal {
  background: var(--bg-secondary);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
  padding: var(--space-xl);
  width: 560px;
  max-width: 100%;
  max-height: 85vh;
  overflow-y: auto;
  box-shadow: var(--glow-blue);
  animation: slideUp 0.3s ease;
}

.modal-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-lg);
}

.modal-header h2 {
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  font-size: 20px;
}

.modal-close {
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1);
  border: none;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  color: var(--danger);
  font-size: 18px;
  cursor: pointer;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  transition: all var(--transition-fast);
}

.modal-close:hover {
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2);
}

/* ============================================
   PROGRESS BARS
   ============================================ */
.progress-bar {
  height: 6px;
  background: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.1);
  border-radius: 3px;
  overflow: hidden;
  margin-top: var(--space-sm);
}

.progress-fill {
  height: 100%;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  border-radius: 3px;
  transition: width var(--transition-slow);
}

/* ============================================
   CHART CONTAINERS
   ============================================ */
.chart-container {
  position: relative;
  height: 300px;
}

/* ============================================
   PIPELINE / KANBAN
   ============================================ */
.pipeline {
  display: flex;
  gap: var(--space-md);
  overflow-x: auto;
  padding-bottom: var(--space-md);
}

.pipeline-column {
  min-width: 280px;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.3);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: var(--space-md);
}

.pipeline-column-title {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-md);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
}

.pipeline-card {
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: var(--space-md);
  margin-bottom: var(--space-sm);
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all var(--transition-fast);
}

.pipeline-card:hover {
  border-color: var(--cyan-holo);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.pipeline-card-title {
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 500;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-xs);
}

.pipeline-card-value {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--cyan-holo);
  font-weight: 600;
}

/* ============================================
   CALENDAR
   ============================================ */
.calendar-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(7, 1fr);
  gap: 4px;
}

.calendar-header-cell {
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 11px;
  color: var(--gray-500);
  padding: 8px;
  font-weight: 600;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.calendar-cell {
  aspect-ratio: 1;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.3);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  padding: 6px;
  font-size: 12px;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all var(--transition-fast);
  position: relative;
}

.calendar-cell:hover {
  border-color: var(--cyan-holo);
}

.calendar-cell.today {
  border-color: var(--blue-electric);
  background: rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.1);
}

.calendar-cell.has-event::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 4px;
  left: 50%;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  background: var(--cyan-holo);
  border-radius: 50%;
}

/* ============================================
   UPLOAD ZONE
   ============================================ */
.upload-zone {
  border: 2px dashed var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  padding: var(--space-2xl);
  text-align: center;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all var(--transition-normal);
}

.upload-zone:hover {
  border-color: var(--cyan-holo);
  background: rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.03);
}

.upload-zone-icon {
  font-size: 48px;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-md);
}

/* ============================================
   TABS
   ============================================ */
.tabs {
  display: flex;
  gap: 4px;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-lg);
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.3);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 4px;
  overflow-x: auto;
}

.tab {
  padding: 10px 20px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  font-size: 13px;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all var(--transition-fast);
  white-space: nowrap;
  color: var(--gray-400);
}

.tab.active {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  color: white;
  font-weight: 500;
}

/* ============================================
   AI CHAT
   ============================================ */
.ai-chat-container {
  height: 400px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.ai-messages {
  flex: 1;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: var(--space-md);
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.3);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  margin-bottom: var(--space-md);
}

.ai-message {
  margin-bottom: var(--space-md);
  display: flex;
  gap: var(--space-md);
}

.ai-message.user {
  flex-direction: row-reverse;
}

.ai-avatar {
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  font-size: 16px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.ai-message.bot .ai-avatar {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
}

.ai-message.user .ai-avatar {
  background: var(--bg-secondary);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
}

.ai-bubble {
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 12px 16px;
  font-size: 14px;
  max-width: 70%;
  line-height: 1.6;
}

.ai-input-row {
  display: flex;
  gap: var(--space-sm);
}

.ai-input-row input {
  flex: 1;
  padding: 12px 16px;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  color: var(--white);
  font-size: 14px;
}

.ai-input-row input:focus {
  outline: none;
  border-color: var(--cyan-holo);
}

/* ============================================
   CALCULATOR RESULT
   ============================================ */
.calc-result {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(45, 127, 249, 0.1), rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.1));
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  padding: var(--space-lg);
  margin-top: var(--space-lg);
}

.calc-result-value {
  font-family: var(--font-title);
  font-size: 36px;
  font-weight: 700;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
  background-clip: text;
}

/* ============================================
   ALERTS
   ============================================ */
.alert-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-md);
  padding: var(--space-md);
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.05);
  border: 1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  margin-bottom: var(--space-sm);
}

.alert-item.warning {
  background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.05);
  border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2);
}

/* ============================================
   MAINTENANCE LOG
   ============================================ */
.maintenance-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: var(--space-md);
  padding: var(--space-md);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
}

.maintenance-dot {
  width: 12px;
  height: 12px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--cyan-holo);
  margin-top: 4px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

/* ============================================
   SEARCH
   ============================================ */
.search-box {
  position: relative;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-lg);
}

.search-box input {
  width: 100%;
  padding: 12px 16px 12px 44px;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  color: var(--white);
  font-size: 14px;
}

.search-box input:focus {
  outline: none;
  border-color: var(--cyan-holo);
}

.search-box .search-icon {
  position: absolute;
  left: 16px;
  top: 50%;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
  color: var(--gray-500);
}

/* ============================================
   TOAST NOTIFICATIONS
   ============================================ */
.toast {
  position: fixed;
  bottom: 24px;
  right: 24px;
  background: var(--bg-secondary);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 16px 24px;
  z-index: 2000;
  display: none;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-md);
  box-shadow: var(--glow-blue);
  animation: slideIn 0.3s ease;
  max-width: 400px;
}

.toast.active {
  display: flex;
}

.toast-icon {
  font-size: 20px;
}

.toast-content {
  flex: 1;
}

.toast-title {
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 14px;
  margin-bottom: 2px;
}

.toast-message {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--gray-400);
}

/* ============================================
   API CONFIG BANNER
   ============================================ */
.api-config-banner {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1), rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1));
  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 16px 20px;
  margin-bottom: var(--space-lg);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: var(--space-md);
}

.api-config-banner.configured {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1), rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.1));
  border-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.3);
}

.api-status {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-md);
  flex: 1;
}

.api-status-dot {
  width: 10px;
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--danger);
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--danger);
}

.api-status-dot.active {
  background: var(--success);
  box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--success);
}

/* ============================================
   AI ENHANCEMENT BADGE
   ============================================ */
.ai-enhancement-badge {
  position: absolute;
  top: 12px;
  right: 12px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--blue-electric), var(--cyan-holo));
  color: white;
  font-size: 10px;
  padding: 4px 10px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  font-weight: 600;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

/* ============================================
   LOADING SPINNER
   ============================================ */
.ai-loading {
  display: inline-block;
  width: 20px;
  height: 20px;
  border: 2px solid rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.3);
  border-top-color: var(--cyan-holo);
  border-radius: 50%;
  animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite;
}

@keyframes spin {
  to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
}

/* ============================================
   HAMBURGER MENU
   ============================================ */
.hamburger {
  display: none;
  position: fixed;
  top: 16px;
  left: 16px;
  z-index: 200;
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 20px;
  color: var(--white);
}

/* ============================================
   ANIMATIONS
   ============================================ */
@keyframes fadeIn {
  from { opacity: 0; }
  to { opacity: 1; }
}

@keyframes slideUp {
  from { 
    opacity: 0; 
    transform: translateY(20px); 
  }
  to { 
    opacity: 1; 
    transform: translateY(0); 
  }
}

@keyframes slideIn {
  from { 
    transform: translateX(100%); 
    opacity: 0; 
  }
  to { 
    transform: translateX(0); 
    opacity: 1; 
  }
}

.page-section {
  display: none;
}

.page-section.active {
  display: block;
  animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease;
}

/* ============================================
   RESPONSIVE
   ============================================ */
@media (max-width: 1200px) {
  .grid-4 { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
  .grid-3 { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .sidebar { 
    transform: translateX(-100%); 
  }
  .sidebar.open { 
    transform: translateX(0); 
  }
  .main-content { 
    margin-left: 0; 
    padding: var(--space-md); 
  }
  .grid-2, .grid-3, .grid-4 { 
    grid-template-columns: 1fr; 
  }
  .stats-grid { 
    grid-template-columns: 1fr; 
  }
  .hamburger { 
    display: flex; 
  }
  .pipeline {
    flex-direction: column;
  }
  .pipeline-column {
    min-width: 100%;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 480px) {
  .top-bar {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: var(--space-md);
  }
  .top-bar-right {
    width: 100%;
    justify-content: flex-end;
  }
}

/* ============================================
   PRINT STYLES
   ============================================ */
@media print {
  .sidebar, .top-bar, .hamburger, .toast { 
    display: none !important; 
  }
  .main-content { 
    margin-left: 0 !important; 
  }
  body { 
    background: white; 
    color: black; 
  }
  .card {
    border: 1px solid #ddd;
    box-shadow: none;
  }
}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="bg-grid"></div>

<!-- LOGIN SCREEN -->
<div class="login-screen" id="loginScreen">
  <div class="login-container">
    <div class="login-logo">FL</div>
    <h1>FazLuiz 3D</h1>
    <p>Plataforma SaaS Universal con IA Gemini</p>
    <input type="text" class="form-input" id="loginUser" placeholder="Usuario" value="thiago" style="margin-bottom:12px">
    <input type="password" class="form-input" id="loginPass" placeholder="Contraseña" value="admin123" style="margin-bottom:20px">
    <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg" style="width:100%" onclick="App.auth.login()">Iniciar Sesión</button>
    <p style="margin-top:16px;font-size:11px;color:var(--gray-500)">v3.0.0 — Powered by Google Gemini AI</p>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- HAMBURGER -->
<div class="hamburger" onclick="App.ui.toggleSidebar()"></div>

<!-- MAIN APP -->
<div class="app-container" id="appContainer">
  
  <!-- SIDEBAR -->
  <nav class="sidebar" id="sidebar">
    <div class="sidebar-logo">
      <div class="sidebar-logo-icon">FL</div>
      <div class="sidebar-logo-text">
        <h2>FazLuiz 3D</h2>
        <span>Thiago Luiz + Gemini AI</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="nav-section">
      <div class="nav-section-title">Principal</div>
      <div class="nav-item active" onclick="App.nav.showPage('dashboard', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">📊</span> Dashboard
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('crm', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">👥</span> CRM
        <span class="nav-badge">12</span>
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('erp', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">🏭</span> ERP / Pedidos
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="nav-section">
      <div class="nav-section-title">Operaciones</div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('calculator', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">🧮</span> Calculadora 3D
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('inventory', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">📦</span> Inventario
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('maintenance', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon"></span> Mantenimiento
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('calendar', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">📅</span> Agenda
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="nav-section">
      <div class="nav-section-title">Finanzas</div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('billing', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">💰</span> Facturación
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('quotes', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">📄</span> Presupuestos
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="nav-section">
      <div class="nav-section-title">Inteligencia Artificial</div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('ai-commercial', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">🤖</span> IA Comercial
        <span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--blue-electric),var(--cyan-holo));padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;color:white">Gemini</span>
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('ai-stl', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">🔬</span> IA Análisis STL
        <span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--blue-electric),var(--cyan-holo));padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;color:white">Gemini</span>
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('ai-business', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">📈</span> IA Empresarial
        <span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--blue-electric),var(--cyan-holo));padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;color:white">Gemini</span>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="nav-section">
      <div class="nav-section-title">Portal</div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('client-portal', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon"></span> Portal Cliente
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('projects', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">🗂️</span> Proyectos
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="nav-section">
      <div class="nav-section-title">Sistema</div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('settings', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon">⚙️</span> Configuración
      </div>
      <div class="nav-item" onclick="App.nav.showPage('security', this)">
        <span class="nav-icon"></span> Seguridad

  <!-- MAIN CONTENT -->
  <main class="main-content">
    
    <!-- TOP BAR -->
    <div class="top-bar">
      <div class="top-bar-left">
        <h1 id="pageTitle">Dashboard</h1>
        <p id="pageSubtitle">Resumen general de FazLuiz 3D</p>
      </div>
      <div class="top-bar-right">
        <button class="top-btn" onclick="App.ui.toggleTheme()" title="Cambiar tema">🌙</button>
        <button class="top-btn" title="Notificaciones">
          🔔<span class="notif-dot"></span>
        </button>
        <div class="user-avatar" title="Thiago Luiz">TL</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- API Status Banner -->
    <div id="apiBanner" class="api-config-banner" style="display:none">
      <div class="api-status">
        <div class="api-status-dot" id="apiStatusDot"></div>
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:600;font-size:14px" id="apiStatusText">API Gemini no configurada</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gray-400)" id="apiStatusSubtext">Modo demo activo</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" onclick="App.nav.showPage('settings', document.querySelector('[onclick*=\"settings\"]'))">Configurar API</button>
    </div>

    <!-- ==================== DASHBOARD ==================== -->
    <div class="page-section active" id="page-dashboard">
      <div class="stats-grid">
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Ingresos del Mes</div>
          <div class="stat-value">€12,450</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">↑ +18.2% vs mes anterior</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Pedidos Activos</div>
          <div class="stat-value">47</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">↑ +5 nuevos esta semana</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Horas Impresas</div>
          <div class="stat-value">328h</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">↑ +12% productividad</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Material Consumido</div>
          <div class="stat-value">45.2 kg</div>
          <div class="stat-change negative">↓ -3% eficiencia</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Beneficio Neto</div>
          <div class="stat-value">€4,820</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">↑ +22.5%</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Clientes Activos</div>
          <div class="stat-value">23</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">↑ +3 nuevos</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">
            <div>
              <div class="card-title">Ingresos vs Gastos</div>
              <div class="card-subtitle">Últimos 6 meses</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="chart-container">
            <canvas id="chartRevenue"></canvas>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">
            <div>
              <div class="card-title">Distribución de Materiales</div>
              <div class="card-subtitle">Consumo actual</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="chart-container">
            <canvas id="chartMaterials"></canvas>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">
            <div>
              <div class="card-title">Pedidos Recientes</div>
              <div class="card-subtitle">Últimos 5 pedidos</div>
            </div>

          </div>
          <div class="table-responsive">

              <thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Cliente</th><th>Estado</th><th>Total</th></tr></thead>

                <tr><td>#1047</td><td>TechCorp SL</td><td><span class="badge badge-success">Completado</span></td><td>€450</td></tr>
                <tr><td>#1046</td><td>DesignStudio</td><td><span class="badge badge-warning">En producción</span></td><td>€280</td></tr>
                <tr><td>#1045</td><td>AutoParts Pro</td><td><span class="badge badge-info">Diseño</span></td><td>€1,200</td></tr>
                <tr><td>#1044</td><td>MedDevice Inc</td><td><span class="badge badge-warning">En producción</span></td><td>€890</td></tr>
                <tr><td>#1043</td><td>ArquiModel</td><td><span class="badge badge-cyan">Pendiente</span></td><td>€340</td></tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">
            <div>
              <div class="card-title">KPIs de Rendimiento</div>
              <div class="card-subtitle">Métricas clave</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div style="margin-bottom:16px">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:4px">
                <span>Rendimiento</span><span style="color:var(--cyan-holo);font-weight:600">98.7%</span>
              </div>
              <div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:98.7%"></div></div>
            </div>
            <div style="margin-bottom:16px">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:4px">
                <span>Eficiencia</span><span style="color:var(--cyan-holo);font-weight:600">96.2%</span>
              </div>
              <div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:96.2%"></div></div>
            </div>
            <div style="margin-bottom:16px">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:4px">
                <span>Precisión</span><span style="color:var(--cyan-holo);font-weight:600">97.3%</span>
              </div>
              <div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:97.3%"></div></div>
            </div>
            <div style="margin-bottom:16px">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:4px">
                <span>Adaptabilidad</span><span style="color:var(--cyan-holo);font-weight:600">94.8%</span>
              </div>
              <div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:94.8%"></div></div>
            </div>
            <div style="margin-bottom:16px">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:4px">
                <span>Versatilidad</span><span style="color:var(--cyan-holo);font-weight:600">95.1%</span>
              </div>
              <div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:95.1%"></div></div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:4px">
                <span>Integridad de Datos</span><span style="color:var(--cyan-holo);font-weight:600">98.7%</span>
              </div>
              <div class="progress-bar"><div class="progress-fill" style="width:98.7%"></div></div>
            </div>

    <!-- ==================== CRM ==================== -->
    <div class="page-section" id="page-crm">
      <div class="tabs">
        <div class="tab active" onclick="App.crm.showTab('pipeline', this)">Embudo</div>

        <div class="tab" onclick="App.crm.showTab('leads', this)">Leads</div>
        <div class="tab" onclick="App.crm.showTab('followup', this)">Seguimiento</div>
      </div>

      <div id="crm-pipeline">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:20px">
          <h3 style="font-family:var(--font-title)">Embudo Comercial</h3>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="App.ui.openModal('modalNewLead')">+ Nuevo Lead</button>

        <div class="pipeline">
          <div class="pipeline-column">

              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gray-400);margin-top:4px">Sector: Tecnología</div>

              <div class="pipeline-card-title">BioMed Solutions</div>
              <div class="pipeline-card-value">€5,000 potencial</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gray-400);margin-top:4px">Sector: Medicina</div>
            </div>
            <div class="pipeline-card">
              <div class="pipeline-card-title">AeroComponents</div>
              <div class="pipeline-card-value">€8,000 potencial</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gray-400);margin-top:4px">Sector: Aeronáutica</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="pipeline-column">
            <div class="pipeline-column-title">
              <span>📞 Contacto</span><span class="badge badge-warning">3</span>
            </div>
            <div class="pipeline-card">
              <div class="pipeline-card-title">DesignStudio Pro</div>
              <div class="pipeline-card-value">€1,800 potencial</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gray-400);margin-top:4px">Email enviado</div>
            </div>
            <div class="pipeline-card">
              <div class="pipeline-card-title">AutoParts Iberia</div>
              <div class="pipeline-card-value">€3,200 potencial</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gray-400);margin-top:4px">Llamada pendiente</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="pipeline-column">
            <div class="pipeline-column-title">
              <span>📋 Propuesta</span><span class="badge badge-cyan">2</span>
            </div>
            <div class="pipeline-card">
              <div class="pipeline-card-title">ArquiModel Madrid</div>
              <div class="pipeline-card-value">€4,500 potencial</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--gray-400);margin-top:4px">Presupuesto enviado</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="pipeline-column">
            <div class="pipeline-column-title">
              <span>✅ Cerrado</span><span class="badge badge-success">4</span>
            </div>
            <div class="pipeline-card">
              <div class="pipeline-card-title">TechCorp SL</div>
              <div class="pipeline-card-value">€12,450 ganado</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--success);margin-top:4px">Cliente activo</div>
            </div>
            <div class="pipeline-card">
              <div class="pipeline-card-title">MedDevice Inc</div>
              <div class="pipeline-card-value">€6,800 ganado</div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--success);margin-top:4px">Contrato firmado</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

      <div id="crm-clients" style="display:none">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:20px">
          <h3 style="font-family:var(--font-title)">Gestión de Clientes</h3>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="App.ui.openModal('modalNewClient')">+ Nuevo Cliente</button>
        </div>
        <div class="search-box">
          <span class="search-icon">🔍</span>
          <input type="text" placeholder="Buscar clientes..." id="clientSearch" oninput="App.crm.filterClients()">
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="table-responsive">
            <table class="data-table">
              <thead><tr><th>Nombre</th><th>Email</th><th>Teléfono</th><th>Sector</th><th>Pedidos</th><th>Total</th><th>Estado</th><th>Acciones</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody id="clientsBody"></tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div id="crm-leads" style="display:none">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:20px">
          <h3 style="font-family:var(--font-title)">Leads</h3>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="App.ui.openModal('modalNewLead')">+ Nuevo Lead</button>
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="table-responsive">
            <table class="data-table">
              <thead><tr><th>Empresa</th><th>Contacto</th><th>Fuente</th><th>Valor</th><th>Probabilidad</th><th>Estado</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                <tr><td>TechVision Labs</td><td>Carlos Méndez</td><td>LinkedIn</td><td>€2,500</td><td>30%</td><td><span class="badge badge-info">Nuevo</span></td></tr>
                <tr><td>BioMed Solutions</td><td>Ana García</td><td>Web</td><td>€5,000</td><td>45%</td><td><span class="badge badge-warning">Contactado</span></td></tr>
                <tr><td>AeroComponents</td><td>Pedro Ruiz</td><td>Referido</td><td>€8,000</td><td>60%</td><td><span class="badge badge-cyan">Propuesta</span></td></tr>
                <tr><td>GreenEnergy Co</td><td>María López</td><td>Feria</td><td>€3,500</td><td>20%</td><td><span class="badge badge-info">Nuevo</span></td></tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div id="crm-followup" style="display:none">
        <h3 style="font-family:var(--font-title);margin-bottom:20px">Seguimiento de Actividades</h3>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="maintenance-item">
            <div class="maintenance-dot"></div>
            <div>
              <div style="font-weight:500;font-size:14px">Llamada con TechCorp SL</div>
              <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gray-400)">Hoy 10:30 — Confirmar pedido #1047</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="maintenance-item">
            <div class="maintenance-dot" style="background:var(--warning)"></div>
            <div>
              <div style="font-weight:500;font-size:14px">Email a DesignStudio Pro</div>
              <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gray-400)">Mañana 09:00 — Enviar presupuesto actualizado</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="maintenance-item">
            <div class="maintenance-dot" style="background:var(--success)"></div>
            <div>
              <div style="font-weight:500;font-size:14px">Reunión con AutoParts Iberia</div>
              <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gray-400)">Viernes 15:00 — Presentación de capacidades</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ==================== ERP ==================== -->
    <div class="page-section" id="page-erp">
      <div class="stats-grid">
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Pedidos Totales</div>
          <div class="stat-value">156</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">↑ +12 este mes</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">En Producción</div>
          <div class="stat-value">8</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">3 urgentes</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Completados</div>
          <div class="stat-value">142</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">91% tasa éxito</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-card">
          <div class="stat-label">Facturación Total</div>
          <div class="stat-value">€89,400</div>
          <div class="stat-change positive">↑ +25% YTD</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card">
        <div class="card-header">
          <div>
            <div class="card-title">Gestión de Pedidos</div>
            <div class="card-subtitle">Control completo de producción</div>
          </div>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="App.ui.openModal('modalNewOrder')">+ Nuevo Pedido</button>
        </div>
        <div class="table-responsive">
          <table class="data-table">
            <thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Cliente</th><th>Descripción</th><th>Material</th><th>Estado</th><th>Inicio</th><th>Entrega</th><th>Total</th><th>Acciones</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody id="ordersBody"></tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">
            <div class="card-title">Estados de Fabricación</div>
          </div>
          <div class="chart-container">
            <canvas id="chartOrderStatus"></canvas>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">
            <div class="card-title">Beneficios por Mes</div>
          </div>
          <div class="chart-container">
            <canvas id="chartProfit"></canvas>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ==================== CALCULADORA 3D ==================== -->
    <div class="page-section" id="page-calculator">
      <div class="grid-2">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">
            <div>
              <div class="card-title">🧮 Calculadora de Impresión 3D</div>
              <div class="card-subtitle">Calcula el precio final automáticamente</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="form-row">
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Gramos de material</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcGrams" value="150" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Precio material (€/kg)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcMaterialPrice" value="25" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="form-row">
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Tiempo de impresión (horas)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcHours" value="8" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Consumo eléctrico (kWh)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcElectricity" value="0.35" step="0.01" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="form-row">
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Precio electricidad (€/kWh)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcElecPrice" value="0.15" step="0.01" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Depreciación máquina (€/h)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcDepreciation" value="2.50" step="0.10" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="form-row">
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Coste mano de obra (€/h)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcLabor" value="15" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Margen de beneficio (%)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcMargin" value="40" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="form-row">
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">Coste post-procesado (€)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcPostProcess" value="5" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
            <div class="form-group">
              <label class="form-label">IVA (%)</label>
              <input type="number" class="form-input" id="calcTax" value="21" oninput="App.calculator.calculate()">
            </div>
          </div>
          <button class="btn btn-primary" style="width:100%;margin-top:8px" onclick="App.calculator.calculate()"> Recalcular</button>
        </div>

        <div>
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title">📊 Desglose de Costes</div>
            </div>
            <div id="calcBreakdown">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border)">
                <span>Material</span><span id="resMaterial" style="font-weight:600">€3.75</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border)">
                <span>Electricidad</span><span id="resElectricity" style="font-weight:600">€0.42</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border)">
                <span>Depreciación</span><span id="resDepreciation" style="font-weight:600">€20.00</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border)">
                <span>Mano de obra</span><span id="resLabor" style="font-weight:600">€120.00</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border)">
                <span>Post-procesado</span><span id="resPostProcess" style="font-weight:600">€5.00</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border);font-weight:600">
                <span>Coste Total</span><span id="resTotalCost" style="color:var(--warning)">€149.17</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border)">
                <span>Margen (40%)</span><span id="resMargin" style="font-weight:600;color:var(--success)">€59.67</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border)">
                <span>Precio sin IVA</span><span id="resPreTax" style="font-weight:600">€208.84</span>
              </div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--glass-border)">
                <span>IVA (21%)</span><span id="resTax" style="font-weight:600">€43.86</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="calc-result">
            <div class="stat-label">PRECIO FINAL AL CLIENTE</div>
            <div class="calc-result-value" id="resFinalPrice">€252.70</div>

            </div>
            <div class="pipeline-card" style="margin-bottom:10px">
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:start">
                <div>
                  <div class="pipeline-card-title">BioMed Solutions</div>
                  <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gray-400)">Buscan proveedor impresión médica</div>
                </div>
                <span class="badge badge-success">Alta (78%)</span>
              </div>
              <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--cyan-holo);margin-top:6px">💰 €5,000-10,000/mes potencial</div>

          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title"> Estadísticas de IA</div>

    <div class="page-section" id="page-ai-stl">

            <div>
              <div class="card-title">🔬 IA Análisis de STL con Gemini</div>
              <div class="card-subtitle">Análisis inteligente de modelos 3D con IA avanzada</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div class="upload-zone" id="stlUploadZone" onclick="document.getElementById('stlFileInput').click()">
            <div class="upload-zone-icon">📁</div>
            <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:500;margin-bottom:8px">Arrastra tu archivo STL aquí</div>
            <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--gray-400)">o haz clic para seleccionar</div>
            <input type="file" id="stlFileInput" accept=".stl,.obj,.3mf" style="display:none" onchange="App.ai.stl.analyze(this)">
          </div>

          <div style="margin-top:16px;padding:16px;background:rgba(45,127,249,0.05);border-radius:12px;border-left:3px solid var(--blue-electric)">
            <div style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.6;color:var(--gray-400)">
              <strong style="color:var(--white)">💡 Análisis potenciado por Gemini:</strong><br>
              • Detección avanzada de errores geométricos<br>
              • Optimización automática de orientación<br>
              • Estimación precisa de costes y tiempos<br>
              • Recomendaciones de material inteligentes
            </div>
          </div>

          <div id="stlAnalysisResult" style="display:none;margin-top:20px">
            <div class="calc-result">
              <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:12px">✅ Análisis IA Completado</div>
              <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px;font-size:13px">
                <div><span style="color:var(--gray-400)">Volumen:</span> <strong id="stlVolume">45.2 cm³</strong></div>
                <div><span style="color:var(--gray-400)">Peso estimado:</span> <strong id="stlWeight">56.5g (PLA)</strong></div>
                <div><span style="color:var(--gray-400)">Tiempo impresión:</span> <strong id="stlTime">6h 24min</strong></div>
                <div><span style="color:var(--gray-400)">Material necesario:</span> <strong id="stlMaterial">62g (+10%)</strong></div>
                <div><span style="color:var(--gray-400)">Soportes:</span> <strong id="stlSupports">12% volumen</strong></div>
                <div><span style="color:var(--gray-400)">Orientación óptima:</span> <strong id="stlOrientation">45° Z</strong></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div style="margin-top:16px">
              <div style="font-weight:600;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:8px">🎯 Recomendaciones IA:</div>
              <div id="stlRecommendations"></div>
            </div>

            <div class="calc-result" style="margin-top:16px">
              <div class="stat-label">COSTE ESTIMADO POR IA</div>
              <div class="calc-result-value" id="stlCost">€28.50</div>
              <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gray-400);margin-top:4px">
                Precio sugerido: <strong style="color:var(--success)" id="stlSuggestedPrice">€45.00</strong>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">

            </div>
            <div class="table-responsive">

<script>
/**
 * FazLuiz 3D - Universal Platform
 * Clean Architecture with Modular Design
 * Version: 3.0.0
 */

// ============================================
// DATA STORE
// ============================================
const Store = {
  clients: [
    { id: 1, name: 'TechCorp SL', email: 'info@techcorp.es', phone: '+34 911 234 567', sector: 'Tecnología', orders: 12, total: 4820, status: 'active' },
```
